Steganographia
Johannes Trithemius (1462-1516) (1608)
Edition staged: Frankfurt: Johannes Berner, 1608 (first published 1606). Latin text from Bayerische Staatsbibliothek scan via Internet Archive (SteganographiaBSB1608).
Source: Internet Archive — https://archive.org/details/SteganographiaBSB1608
Rights: Public Domain. Author died 1516; edition published 1608, well before 1930.
Language: Latin
Staged for the Tower of Babel digital library (antarctic-labs.com).
Note: No public-domain English translation exists (McLean 1982 and Skinner/Clark editions are in copyright). This is the complete Latin text (Books I-III with Clavis). OCR of early-modern print; expect period spelling and OCR noise.
